# 1. Citation................................................................................................................2
Citation.
This Order may be cited as the Parliamentary Elections (Polling Divisions)
Order, 2026.
2. Polling Division Boundaries Redrawn.
The boundaries of the polling divisions into which the constituencies set out in
the First, Second and Third Schedules to the House of Assembly Revision of
Boundaries and Re-distribution of Seats Order, 2026 being the constituencies in
the Island of New Providence, in the Island of Grand Bahama and in the
remainder of the Family Islands, respectively as divided shall be redrawn in
accordance with the First, Second and Third Schedules.
3. Revokes S.I. No. 27 of 2017.
The Parliamentary Elections (Polling Divisions) Order, 2017 is revoked. FIRST SCHEDULE
(paragraph 2)
